                                                                                                        Definitions
                                                                                                              釋義

In this report, the following expressions shall have the following meanings     於本報告內，除文義另有所指外，下列詞彙
unless the context indicates otherwise:                                         具有以下涵義：


“Aggregate Trade Balance”                   the total balances of the net trade receivables, net bills receivable and acceptance
                                            bills which are received from the customers to settle their trade debts
「貿易應收總款項」                                   貿易應收賬款淨額、應收票據淨額及承兌票據款項（從客戶收取以結算其貿易
                                            債務）之總額

“AGM”                                       the annual general meeting of the Company or any adjournment thereof
「股東週年大會」                                    本公司股東週年大會或其任何續會

“Articles”                                  the articles of association of the Company, as amended, supplemented, modified
                                            or otherwise adopted from time to time
「章程細則」                                      本公司不時修訂、補充、修改或以其他方式採納的章程細則

“Audit Committee”                           the audit committee of the Company
「審核委員會」                                     本公司的審核委員會

“Auto-finance Services”                     the provision of financing to the end-users and dealers of the Group’s products
                                            for the purpose of purchasing the Group’s vehicles
「汽車金融服務」                                    向本集團產品的終端用戶及經銷商就購買本集團的車輛而提供融資

“Award Share(s)”                            the Shares to be granted under the Share Award Scheme
「激勵股份」                                      根據股份激勵計劃可授予的股份

“Board”                                     the board of Directors
「董事會」                                       董事會

“CAAM”                                      China Association of Automobile Manufacturers
「中國汽車工業協會」                                  中國汽車工業協會

“Chairman”                                  the chairman of the Board
「董事長」                                       董事會主席

“China” or “PRC” or “Mainland China”        the People’s Republic of China, and for the purpose of this report, excludes Hong
                                            Kong, the Macao Special Administrative Region of the PRC and Taiwan
「中國」或「中國大陸」                                 中華人民共和國（就本報告而言，並不包括香港、澳門特別行政區及台灣）

“CNHTC”                                     中國重型汽車集團有限公司(China National Heavy Duty Truck Group Company
                                            Limited), a state-owned enterprise organized under the laws of the PRC with
                                            limited liability, being the immediate holding company of the Company
「中國重汽」                                      中國重型汽車集團有限公司，一家根據中國法律組成的國有有限責任公司，
                                            為本公司的直接控股公司

“CNHTC Group”                               CNHTC and its subsidiaries other than the Group
「中國重汽集團」                                    中國重汽及其附屬公司，不包括本集團

“Commercial Lending Services”               the provision of loans to the borrowers, bill discounting services for bank bills
                                            presented by the borrowers and issue of bills (off-balance sheet credit business)
「商業貸款服務」                                    向借款人提供貸款、為借款人提交的銀行票據提供票據貼現服務及發行票據
                                           （表外信貸業務）

    Definitions
    釋義


    “Companies Ordinance”             the Companies Ordinance (Chapter 622 of the Laws of Hong Kong)
    「公司條例」                            香港法例第 622 章公司條例

    “Company”or “Sinotruk”            Sinotruk (Hong Kong) Limited, a company incorporated in Hong Kong with
                                      limited liability, and the shares of which are listed on the Main Board of the Stock
                                      Exchange
    「本公司」或「重汽香港」                      中國重汽（香港）有限公司，一家在香港註冊成立的有限公司並且其股份在聯
                                      交所主板上市

    “Director(s)”                     the director(s) of the Company
    「董事」                              本公司董事

    “ED(s)”                           the executive Director(s)
    「執行董事」                            執行董事

    “Engines Segment”                 the engines segment of the Group which engages in manufacture and sale of
                                      engines, gearboxes and related parts
    「發動機分部」                           本集團發動機分部，從事製造及銷售發動機、變速箱及相關零部件

    “ESG”                             environmental, social and governance
    「ESG」                             環境、社會及企業管治

    “ESG Committee”                   the environmental, social and governance committee of the Company
    「ESG 委員會」                         本公司的環境、社會及管治委員會

    “Euro”                            the lawful currency of the European Union
    「歐元」                              歐盟的法定貨幣歐元

    “Executive Committee”             the executive committee of the Company
    「執行委員會」                           本公司的執行委員會

    “Finance Segment”                 the finance segment of the Group which engages in provision of deposit taking,
                                      Commercial Lending Services and entrustment loans to the members of the
                                      Group and members of CNHTC Group as well as the provision of Auto-financing
                                      Services and supply chain financing services to the public
    「金融分部」                            本集團金融分部，從事向本集團成員公司及中國重汽集團成員公司提供存
                                      款、商業貸款服務和委託貸款，以及向大眾提供汽車金融服務及供應鏈金融
                                      服務

    “FPFPS”                           Ferdinand Porsche Familien-Privatstiftung, an Austrian private foundation
                                      (Privatstifung) (trust), being the beneficiary owner of 25% of the entired issued
                                      share capital of the Company plus 1 Share
    「FPFPS」                           Ferdinand Porsche Familien-Privatstiftung，一家奧地利私人基金會並實益持
                                      有本公司已發行股份的 25% 加一股股份

    “FPFPS Group”                     FPFPS and its subsidiaries including Volkswagen AG and TRATON SE
    「FPFPS 集團」                        FPFPS 及其附屬公司包括大眾汽車和 TRATON SE

    “GAAP”                            generally accepted accounting principles
    「公認會計原則」                          公認會計原則

「重卡」                                       重型卡車及中重型卡車

“HDTs Segment” or                          the heavy duty trucks segment of the Group which engages in manufacture and
  “Heavy Duty Trucks Segment”              sale of heavy duty trucks, medium-heavy duty trucks and related components
「重卡分部」                                     本集團重卡分部，從事製造及銷售重型卡車、中重型卡車及相關零部件

「港元」                                       香港的法定貨幣港元

「獨立非執行董事」                                  獨立非執行董事

“LDT(s)”                                   light duty truck(s)
「輕卡」                                       輕型卡車

“LDTs and Others Segment” or               the light duty trucks and others segment of the Group which engages in
  “Light Duty Trucks and Others Segment”   manufacture and sale of light duty trucks, buses, other vehicles and related
                                           components
「輕卡及其他分部」                                  本集團輕卡及其他分部，從事製造及銷售輕卡、客車、其他車輛及相關零部
                                           件

“Listing Rules”                            the Rules Governing the Listing of Securities on the Stock Exchange
「上市規則」                                     聯交所證券上市規則

“NED(s)”                                   the non-executive Director(s)
「非執行董事」                                    非執行董事

“Nomination Committee”                     the nomination committee of the Company
「提名委員會」                                    本公司的提名委員會

“Operating Profit (Loss) Margin”           the ratio of operating profit (loss) to revenue of the segment of the Group
「經營溢利（虧損）率」                                本集團分部的經營溢利（虧損）與收入之比率

“PBOC”                                     The People’s Bank of China
「中國人民銀行」                                   中國人民銀行

“Period”                                   the six-month period ended 30 June 2025
「回顧期」                                      截至 2025 年 6 月 30 日止 6 個月

「總裁」                                       本公司總裁

                                                                                 Definitions
                                                                                        釋義


“Subsidiary”         a subsidiary for the time being of the Company within the meaning of the
                     Companies Ordinance whether incorporated in Hong Kong or elsewhere and
                     “Subsidiaries” shall be construed accordingly
「附屬公司」               具有公司條例中涵義的本公司當時的附屬公司（不論於香港或其他地區註冊成
                     立）而「附屬公司」之眾數形式亦應據此解釋

“TRATON SE”          a company incorporated under the laws of Germany with limited liability, being
                     an indirect non-wholly owned subsidiary of FPFPS and the shares of which are
                     listed on the Frankfurt Stock Exchange in Germany and NASDAQ STOCKHOLM
                     (stock code: ISIN DE000TRAT0N7, WKN TRAT0N and symbol 8TRA)
「TRATON SE」          一家根據德國法律註冊成立的有限公司並為 FPFPS 的間接非全資附屬公司，
                     其股份於德國法蘭克福證券交易所及 NASDAQ STOCKHOLM 上市（股份代號：
                     ISIN DE000TRAT0N7，WKN TRAT0N 及代號 8TRA）

“USD”                United States dollars, the lawful currency of the United States of America
「美元」                 美國的法定貨幣美元

“Volkswagen AG”      Volkswagen AG, a company incorporated under the laws of Germany with limited
                     liability, being an indirect non-wholly owned subsidiary of FPFPS and the shares
                     of which are listed on Frankfurt Stock Exchange in Germany (stock code: ISIN
                     DE0007664005, WKN 766400 and symbol VOW)
「大眾汽車」               Volkswagen AG（大眾汽車），一家根據德國法律註冊成立的有限公司並為
                     FPFPS 的間接非全資附屬公司，其股份於德國法蘭克福證券交易所上市（股份
                     代號：ISIN DE0007664005, WKN 766400 及代號 VOW）

“Volkswagen Group”   Volkswagen AG and its subsidiaries
「大眾汽車集團」             大眾汽車及其附屬公司

“Weichai Power”      濰柴動力股份有限公司 (Weichai Power Co., Ltd.), a company organized under
                     the laws of the PRC with limited liability and the shares of which are listed on
                     the Main Board of the Stock Exchange (stock code: 02338) and on the Shenzhen
                     Stock Exchange (stock code: 000338)
「濰柴動力」               濰柴動力股份有限公司，一家根據中國法律註冊成立的有限公司，其股份於
                     聯交所主板（股份代號：02338）及深交所（股份代號：000338）上市

「同比」                 與上年同期比較

     The Group
     集團概況

     Business                                                                           業務
     The Group is one of the leading trucks manufacturers in the PRC which              本集團為中國領先卡車製造商之一，專營研
     specializes in the research, development and manufacture of HDTs,                  發及製造重卡、中重卡、輕卡等以及相關主
     medium-heavy duty trucks, LDTs, etc. and related key assemblies, parts and         要總成及零部件。本集團產品廣泛服務於物
     components. The Group’s products widely serve customer groups in various           流、交通運輸和基礎設施建設等不同行業和
     industries and fields such as logistics, transportation and infrastructure         領域的客戶群。
     construction.


     The Group mainly manufactures trucks and also produces key assemblies,             本集團以整車製造為龍頭，並自製發動機、
     parts and components such as engines, cabins, axles, steel frames, gearboxes,      駕駛室、車橋、車架及變速箱等關鍵零部件
     etc. The Group is a truck manufacturer which has its own research and              及總成，是具有自主研發和製造能力、產業
     development and production capability in trucks as well as the complete            鏈完整的卡車製造商。本集團產品不僅在國
     production chain. Our products are not only sold domestically but also exported    內銷售，還向世界其它國家和地區銷售。此
     to other countries and regions in the world. In addition, the Group provides       外，本集團對本集團產品生產和銷售相關人
     financial services to those parties related to the production and sales of the     士及中國重汽集團提供金融服務。
     Group’s products and to the CNHTC Group.

                                                Management Discussion and Analysis
                                                             管理層討論與分析

MARKET REVIEW                                                                     市場回顧
TRUCKS MARKET                                                                     卡車市場
In the first half of the year, the Chinese government adhered to the general      上半年，中國政府堅持穩中求進工作總基
principle of seeking progress while maintaining stability and coordinated         調，統籌國內經濟工作和國際經貿鬥爭，有
domestic economic work with international economic and trade engagements,         效實施更加積極有為的宏觀政策，國民經濟
effectively implementing more proactive and impactful macro policies. The         迎難而上，總體運行穩中有進，新質生產力
national economy forged ahead amidst challenges, maintaining overall stable       成長壯大，高質量發展扎實推進，主要經濟
operations with qualitative improvements. New quality productive forces           指標表現良好，社會大局保持穩定。上半年
expanded significantly, high-quality development advanced steadily, key           國內生產總值同比增長 5.3%，全國規模以
economic indicators demonstrated robust performance, and overall social           上工業增加值同比增長 6.4%，全國固定資
stability was sustained. China’s GDP grew by 5.3% YoY in the first half of the    產投資（不含農戶）同比增長 2.8%，製造業
year. The total value added of industrial enterprises above the designated size   投資同比增長 7.5%。中國經濟延續穩中向
grew by 6.4% YoY. The national investment in fixed assets (excluding rural        好發展態勢，展現出強大的韌性和活力。
households) grew by 2.8% YoY, the investment in manufacturing grew by 7.5%.
China’s economy continues to develop steadily, showing strong resilience and
vitality.

During the Period, the Ministry of Transport, the National Development            回顧期內，交通運輸部、國家發展和改革委
and Reform Commission, and the Ministry of Finance issued the Notice on           員會和財政部發佈《關於實施老舊營運貨車
Implementing the Retirement and Renewal of Obsolete Commercial Trucks             報廢更新的通知》，推動「國四」老舊營運貨
(《關於實施老舊營運貨車報廢更新的通知》), which promoted the retiring                                車報廢更新，有效刺激了國內商用車市場，
and renewal of old “National IV” emission standard commercial vehicles and        商用車行業呈現持續向好態勢。重卡行業方
effectively stimulated the domestic commercial vehicle market, leading to         面，一是新能源重卡經濟性不斷凸顯，銷量
sustained improvement within the commercial vehicle industry. For the heavy       同 比 增 長 185.9%， 滲 透 率 達 到 22.8%， 銷
duty truck industry, firstly, the cost-effectiveness of new energy heavy duty     量和滲透率持續攀升；二是出口市場需求旺
trucks has become increasingly prominent, with the sales volume increased         盛，隨著中國重卡品牌認可度不斷提升，
by 185.9% YoY and the market penetration reached approximately 22.8%, both        出口銷量再創歷史新高。根據中國汽車工
sales volume and market penetration continuing to climb; secondly, with the       業協會統計，上半年實現重卡銷售 53.92 萬
improvement of brand awareness of heavy duty trucks in China, the export          輛， 同 比 增 長 6.88%， 其 中 出 口 銷 售 15.56

reached approximately 539,200 units in the first half of the year, representing   消費持續發揮經濟增長主引擎作用，社會消
an increase of 6.88% YoY, of which export sales accounted for 155,600 units,      費品零售總額同比增長 5%，有力支撐輕卡

its group. Subsequently, on 11 November 2024, the Board resolved that                    11 日，董事會決定中國重汽財務有限公司

Liquidation”).

been gradually reducing all its business including not to renew the existing             務有限公司逐步減少其所有業務運營，其中

RISK MANAGEMENT POLICY AND KEY INTERNAL CONTROL MEASURES                            風險管理政策及關鍵內部控制措施
Credit approval process and credit risk assessment policy                           信貸審批流程與信貸風險評估政策
Prior to the granting of financial services to the borrowers, the relevant          在向借款人提供金融服務之前，金融分部相
business units (“Business Unit(s)”) of the Finance Segment will first review the    關業務部門（「業務部門」）將首先審核潛在借
application of the potential borrower, and conduct appropriate pre-loan or pre-     款人之申請，並對潛在借款人及其擔保人進
lease checks on the potential borrower and its guarantor, which involves (a)        行適當貸前或租前審查，包括 (a) 審核潛在
reviewing the financial reports and statements of the potential borrower; and (b)   借款人之財務報告及報表；及 (b) 對潛在借
performing an assessment on the financial condition of the potential borrower       款人及其權益持有人（針對實體）之財務狀況
and its equity holder(s) (for entities), such as the type and value of assets       進行評估，如潛在借款人所擁有之資產類型
owned by the potential borrower.                                                    及價值。

Depending on the type and amount of the financing services, the Business Units      根據融資服務種類及金額，業務部門將於考
will assess and decide the necessity and the amount of security/collateral          慮包括但不限於還款歷史、對借款人公開信
for the granting of each loan or lease on a case by case basis considering the      譽查詢之結果、借款人所擁有之資產價值及
factors including but not limited to the repayment history, results of public       位置以及借款人財務狀況等因素後，根據具
credit search towards the borrower, the value and location of the assets owned      體情況評估及決定提供各項貸款或租賃之必
by the borrower and the financial condition of the borrower.                        要性及擔保 / 抵押品金額。

Relevant business approval forms including but not limited to details of the        本集團將編製包括但不限於金額、還款條款
amounts, repayment terms and the applicable interest rate will be prepared          及適用利率等詳情之相關業務審批表格，並
and the senior management of the relevant Business Unit will give final             由相關業務部門之高級管理層就相關申請作
approval in respect of the relevant application and, pursuant to which, the         出最終批准，據此，業務部門將執行相關的
Business Unit will execute the relevant drawdown or payment procedures.             提款或付款程序。

and report any material defaulted loans immediately upon occurrence. In             告。此外，本集團就借款人財務狀況及抵押
addition, the Group carries out regular and/or specific inspections in respect of   品狀況進行定期及 / 或特定檢查。
the financial status of the borrowers and the status of the collaterals.

pursuant to which, depending on the status of the overdue payment, the              此，根據逾期付款的支付情況，業務部門將
Business Units will continuously contact the borrower via different means           繼續通過電話、面談等多種方式與借款人聯

     in assessing the relevant risks of loss in respect of the financing receivables    相關損失風險時，金融分部至少每季度對相

The principal risks faced by the Group and the mitigation measures taken             本集團在回顧期內面臨的主要風險及應對措
during the Period are as follows:                                                    施如下：

1.   QUALITY CONTROL RISKS                                                           1.   質量管控風險
      Quality is the lifeline of the development of the heavy duty truck industry.        質量是重卡行業發展的生命線。本集
      Starting with quality system control, research and development quality              團從質量體系管控、研發質量管理、

      the Group comprehensively advances the achievement of “Reduction of                 面推進「整車 3MIS 降低」質量風控目標
      3MIS for Complete Trucks” quality risk control target and strictly controls         實現，嚴控質量風險。
      quality risks.

      conformity-based to vector-based evaluation. Secondly, we continuously              升級；二是持續開展研發質量管理過
      controlled R&D quality regulatory procedures, determined the quality                程控制，通過自助分析平台完成各類

      avoidance items of similar vehicle models in newly developed projects, to           項目同類車型的問題規避項收集、分

GOING CONCERN                                                                       持續經營
Based on the current financial forecast and the funding that can be utilized, the   根據現行財務預測和可動用的融資情況，本
Group will have sufficient financial resources to continue its operations in the    集團在可預見未來有充足財務資源以持續經
foreseeable future. As a result, the financial statements were prepared based       營。因此，在編製財務報表時已基於持續經
on the going concern assumption.                                                    營基準編製。

DISCLAIMER ON NON-GAAP FINANCIAL MEASURES                                           非公認會計原則指標的免責聲明
Affiliated export revenue is a non-GAAP financial measure and it is used for        聯營出口收入為非公認會計原則指標，該指
assessing the Group’s actual export performance. The Group treats the type of       標用於評估本集團的實際出口表現。本集團
sales to dealers who purchase the Group’s products and directly export to their     將經銷商購買本集團產品後直接出口至其海
overseas customers as our export sales. Hence, it may not be comparable to          外客戶的銷售類型視為出口銷售。因此，聯
similarly titled measures for other companies and should not be considered          營出口收入未必可與其他公司的同類指標作
as an alternative to revenue by geographical markets as an indicator of the         比較，也不應被視作地區市場收入（本集團
operating performance of the Group.                                                 業務指標）的替補。

The use of non-GAAP financial measure is provided solely to enhance                 非公認會計準則指標的提供僅為加強投資者
the overall understanding of the Group’s current financial performance.             對本集團現時財務表現的整體理解。此外，
Additionally, since the Group has historically reported non-GAAP results            由於本集團以往也向投資者報告若干非公認
to investors, it is considered the inclusion of non-GAAP financial measure          會計原則指標以反映本集團業績，因此本集
provides consistency in the Group’s financial reporting.                            團認為非公認會計原則指標可使本集團的財
                                                                                    務報告保持一致性。

     an appropriate balance of skills, experience and perspectives, so as to enhance    能、經驗以及視角的多元化方面達到適當的
     the effective operation of the Board and maintain high standards of corporate      平衡，從而提升董事會的有效運作，保持高
     governance. It also sets measurable objectives for selecting candidates based      標準的企業管治水平。參考本公司的業務模
     on a range of diverse perspectives, taking into account the business model and     式和特定需求，基於一系列多元化視角，制
     specific needs of the Company, including but not limited to gender, age, race,     定甄選候選人的可量化指標，包括但不限於
     language, cultural background, educational background, industry experience         性別、年齡、種族、語言、文化背景、教育
     and professional experience.                                                       背景、行業經驗和專業經驗。

The Share Award Scheme for the purpose of (i) retaining and attracting talents      股份激勵計劃旨在：(i) 通過優化薪酬架構以
through an enhanced remuneration structure; (ii) better aligning the interests      留住及吸引人才；(ii) 更好地將高級人員、
of the officers and employees, and of the Shareholders; and (iii) effectively       員工及股東的利益結合在一起；及 (iii) 有效
motivating the management team and key employees of the Group, and                  地激勵本集團管理團隊及主要員工，從而促
thereby promoting the long-term development of the Group and maximising             進本集團的長遠發展並使股東利益最大化。
the interests of the Shareholders. The grantees to the Share Award Scheme           本次股份激勵計劃授予的激勵對象包括董
include the Directors, senior management, and core research and development,        事、高級管理層、核心研發技術、營銷、管
technical, sales and management employees.                                          理骨幹員工。

(representing approximately 0.98% of the total issued Shares) to 188 grantees        劃向包括 4 名執行董事及其他高級管理層在
including 4 executive Directors and other senior management under the Share          內的 188 名被授予人授予合計 27,170,000 股
Award Scheme. The Company had received the grant price monies of all Award           激勵股份（約佔已發行股份總數的 0.98%）。

                                                                                     款。

     Certified Public Accountants. A review of interim financial report consists of       包括主要向負責財務和會計事務的人員作出
     making inquiries, primarily of persons responsible for financial and accounting      查詢，及實施分析性程序和其他審閱程序。
     matters, and applying analytical and other review procedures. A review is            審閱範圍遠小於根據香港審計準則進行之審
     substantially less in scope than an audit conducted in accordance with Hong          計，故此我們未能保證可知悉所有在審計中

1   BASIS OF PREPARATION                                                          1   編製基準
    Sinotruk (Hong Kong) Limited (the “Company”) was incorporated in Hong             因中國重型汽車集團有限公司（「中國
    Kong on 31 January 2007 as a limited liability company as a result of a           重 汽 」）進 行 集 團 重 組， 中 國 重 汽（香
    group reorganization of China National Heavy Duty Truck Group Company             港 ）有 限 公 司（「本 公 司 」）於 2007 年 1
    Limited (“CNHTC”). The address of the Company’s registered office                 月 31 日在香港註冊成立為有限公司。
    is Units 2102-03, China Merchants Tower, Shun Tak Centre, 168-200                 本公司的註冊辦事處地址為香港干諾
    Connaught Road Central, Hong Kong. The Company’s shares are listed on             道中 168-200 號信德中心招商局大廈
    the Main Board of The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited.                        2102-03 室。 本 公 司 的 股 份 在 香 港 聯
                                                                                      合交易所有限公司的證券交易主板市
                                                                                      場上市。


    The Company and its subsidiaries are hereinafter collectively referred            本公司連同其附屬公司（統稱為「本集
    to as the Group. The Group is principally engaged in the research,                團」）專營研發及製造重卡、中重卡、
    development and manufacture of heavy duty trucks, medium-heavy                    輕 卡 等， 及 其 發 動 機、 駕 駛 室、 車
    duty trucks, light duty trucks, etc. and related key assemblies, parts            橋、車架及變速箱等關鍵零部件和總
    and components including engines, cabins, axles, steel frames and                 成以及提供金融服務。
    gearboxes, and the provision of financial services.

      25 MATERIAL RELATED PARTY TRANSACTIONS                                              25 關聯方交易
          The immediate holding company of the Company is China National Heavy              中國重型汽車集團有限公司（「中國重
          Duty Truck Group Company Limited (“CNHTC”), a company incorporated                汽」），於中國註冊成立，為本集團的
          in Mainland China. The ultimate holding company of the Company is                 直接控股公司。本集團的最終控股公
